# Agents · F · part 16

945 entries, frontend-code-reviewer to frontend-developer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **frontend-code-reviewer**: フロントエンドコードレビューの専門エージェント。フロントエンドエンジニアが実装したコードの品質・設計をレビューする。git logで変更内容を確認し、Beadsタスクの要件と照合してOK/NGを判定する。 ([smallpiece2024/two-device-translator](https://github.com/smallpiece2024/two-device-translator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/frontend-code-reviewer.md) · 8 copies)
- **frontend-code-reviewer**: Reviews frontend code (Next.js 16 App Router, Cache Components, React Server/Client Components, TypeScript, Tailwind) in this repo for compile errors, messy/unstructured code, and misuse of Next.js 16's cache system, then previews the findings and waits for the user to explicitly say "refactor" before fixing anything. Use when the user asks to review, audit, or check the quality of frontend code, or after frontend changes when they want error-checking and a structure/performance review. Do not use for backend (Express/Prisma) code. ([ThantThureinHtut/sarlartal](https://github.com/ThantThureinHtut/sarlartal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/frontend-code-reviewer.md))
- **frontend-code-reviewer**: Reviews frontend code (Next.js/React/TypeScript) against best practices, project conventions, naming standards, performance, accessibility, and security.
